SECTION 1 RECENT NEWS & PAST YEAR TOP 10 STORIES

1.1 RECENT NEWS

Recent News 1 of 2, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 310 words

Mon Oct 19, 2026: Kingspan Group Expands DeLand Headquarters with Sustainable Retrofit

Kingspan Group plc has completed a significant expansion of its North American headquarters in DeLand, Florida, transforming the facility into a 29,000-square-foot space. This project, finalized on 19th October 2026, has introduced new offices, shared workstations, and a dedicated client experience area to the campus that has served as Kingspan's operational base for over a decade.

The retrofitting effort was notably driven by sustainability objectives, with an emphasis on environmental responsibility throughout the construction process. The newly expanded facility features an all-electric design, eliminating the use of natural gas systems, and includes 12 electric vehicle charging stations. As reported by The Business Journals, this expansion is part of Kingspan's strategy to enhance its operational footprint in North America.

Substantial physical improvements were made during the retrofit, including the installation of over 21,000 square feet of insulated metal wall panels and 30,000 square feet of insulated metal roof panels. In addition to new office spaces, the project has created break rooms and shared workstations, along with an updated client experiential area, as detailed by perusglassmag.com.

In terms of sustainability metrics, Kingspan's decision to retain much of the existing structure has led to a 60% reduction in embodied carbon compared to a complete demolition and rebuild. Approximately 99% of construction waste was diverted from landfills through recycling, reuse, or donation. The headquarters is on track to achieve LEED v4 BD+C Gold Certification and the International Living Future Institute's Net Zero Carbon + Energy Certification, according to roofingcontractor.com.

Since establishing its North American hub in DeLand in 2013, Kingspan Group has consistently invested in enhancing its operations in the region. Alongside the DeLand expansion, the company has also inaugurated a new manufacturing facility in Mattoon, Illinois, indicating a broader strategy to bolster manufacturing capacity beyond its Florida base. The investment in both locations underscores Kingspan's commitment to growth within the North American market.

Recent News 2 of 2, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 284 words

Fri Aug 14, 2026: Kingspan Group plc Completes Landmark Acquisition of BMC Manufacturing

Kingspan Group plc has completed the acquisition of BMC Manufacturing in a landmark transaction valued at €900 million. This acquisition is significant as it marks the largest acquisition of a private Irish company by another Irish firm, highlighting the robust capabilities of Irish enterprises in the competitive M&A landscape.

The deal was officially announced on the 14th of August, 2026, and underscores the strategic growth ambitions of Kingspan Group plc, a global leader in high-performance insulation and building envelope solutions. BMC Manufacturing specializes in designing and manufacturing low voltage switchgear and critical power management solutions for the data centre industry.

James Toomey, M&A Advisory Lead at Deloitte, who facilitated the transaction, commented, "This is a landmark transaction as the largest ever acquisition of a private Irish company by an Irish buyer. It reflects the strength of Ireland Inc. that two Irish companies can transact at this scale." He further added that the partnership with Kingspan will significantly benefit BMC Manufacturing, accelerating its growth plans, particularly in the US market.

Deloitte played a pivotal role in this transaction, serving as the lead M&A advisor to the shareholders of BMC Manufacturing. The advisory firm provided comprehensive services across various domains, including financial vendor due diligence and tax structuring, ensuring a smooth transaction process. Brendan Meehan, Founder and Chairman of BMC Manufacturing, acknowledged the invaluable support from Deloitte, stating that their expertise facilitated navigating the complexities of the deal.

The acquisition, pending regulatory approval, is expected to finalize in the fourth quarter of 2026. This development not only represents a significant milestone for Kingspan Group plc but also sets a precedent for further M&A activity within the Irish market, showcasing the resilience and ambition of domestic companies.

1.2 PAST YEAR TOP 10 STORIES

Story 1 of 10, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 70 words

Data-centre power specialist acquired in transaction of up to 900 million euro

Alliance News and RTE reported an initial 850 million euro purchase of BMC Manufacturing, plus up to 50 million euro contingent, funded by 600 million euro cash and 250 million euro of new shares. BMC 2026 revenue is indicated near 280 million euro and EBITDA near 90 million euro. Completion is expected in late 2026 subject to clearance. The deal extends Advnsys into power systems, with integration and earn-out risk.

Story 2 of 10, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 73 words

Record full-year revenue of 9.2 billion euro and trading profit of 955 million euro

Official results disclosed revenue of 9.2 billion euro, up 7 percent, and trading profit of 955 million euro, up 5 percent. Advnsys sales rose 12 percent with backlog 24 percent ahead. Acquisitions added 8 percent of sales growth. Trading margin eased to 10.4 percent and net debt rose to 1,884 million euro. Management indicated a path toward about 1.05 billion euro of trading profit in the following year, subject to mixed construction demand.

Story 3 of 10, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 65 words

First-half profit rises 10 percent as full-year targets are lifted toward 10 billion euro of sales

RTE reported first-half revenue of 4.858 billion euro, up 8 percent, and trading profit of 487 million euro, up 10 percent. Advnsys sales were indicated up 34 percent. Management stated that full-year revenue can reasonably exceed 10 billion euro and lifted trading-profit guidance to about 1.125 billion euro. Currency and listing-review costs reduced the print. Second-half delivery is assessed as critical to the raised target.

Story 4 of 10, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 58 words

Minority listing of data-infrastructure unit considered on Euronext Amsterdam

An official announcement and Reuters reporting indicated consideration of a 25 percent Amsterdam listing of Advnsys while retaining majority control and consolidation. Advnsys 2024 EBITDA was cited at 197 million euro on 1.48 billion euro of revenue. Davy and Goodbody commentary pointed to a possible 6 billion euro unit value. The plan was exploratory, market-dependent and later withdrawn.

Story 5 of 10, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 65 words

Planned minority listing of data-infrastructure unit withdrawn in favour of full ownership

Reuters and RTE reported that the Advnsys offering would not proceed. Management cited stronger momentum toward at least doubling core profit over four to five years and a preference to keep the order book inside the group. The Annual Report treated the decision as a non-adjusting subsequent event. Holders retain full upside and full capex burden, forgoing a minority listing that had drawn investor interest.

Story 6 of 10, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 60 words

Construction starts on 280 million euro manufacturing campus near Lviv

The company newsroom announced a first permit for a 280 million euro low-carbon campus near Lviv, expected to support more than 700 jobs within a five-year build. Output is indicated as insulation, building materials and district heating for reconstruction. UkraineInvest and project counsel confirmed government engagement. Security, permitting and timeline risk remain high. Near-term earnings contribution is assessed as limited.

Story 7 of 10, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 63 words

Remaining shares in Nordic waterproofing platform taken private after 90 percent threshold

Irish Times and Irish Examiner reporting, plus the official offer outcome, showed the holding rise through 90 percent to 98.14 percent, triggering squeeze-out and delisting. Annual Report total consideration was 451.6 million euro including acquired debt. Nordic is a Northern European waterproofing platform with about 1,300 staff. Nordic construction weakness and modest historic operating profit frame the return risk on the accumulated outlay.

Story 8 of 10, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 60 words

Central European daylighting and smoke-ventilation operations added for 99.7 million euro

Group accounts disclosed a 99.7 million euro combined price for Mercor daylighting and smoke-ventilation activities completed in October 2025 across eight countries. The seller cited a maximum 420 million zloty including a contingent slice. The deal was not individually material to group results. It is interpreted as an Advnsys European bolt-on with integration and earn-out measurement risk through March 2026.

Story 9 of 10, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 66 words

Majority holding taken in Argentine structural insulated-panel manufacturer

RTE reported a first Argentine acquisition via a majority stake in Grupo LTN, a panel maker with more than 377 staff and plants in Mendoza and Buenos Aires. Latin America was indicated at about 1,700 employees and 500 million euro of annualised revenue. Price and LTN earnings were not disclosed. The move is assessed as a coherent local manufacturing entry with elevated macro and currency risk.

Story 10 of 10, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 64 words

First-quarter sales hold up as data-infrastructure unit rises 28 percent

The April trading update, as reported by RTE and the Irish Examiner, showed sales of 2.1 billion euro, up 3 percent pre-currency, despite winter disruption. Advnsys sales were up 28 percent pre-currency. Envelope sales were 2 percent behind. Guidance for full-year trading profit remained about 1.05 billion euro, with Kentucky and United States roofing plants still on schedule. Mix quality was not fully disclosed.

SECTION 2 CORPORATE PROFILE

2.1 Activities

Kingspan Group plc, an Irish-based multinational, is a leading provider of high-performance insulation and building envelope solutions, specializing in energy-efficient products for residential, commercial, and industrial applications. The company's core activities encompass the design, manufacture, and distribution of insulation panels, roofing systems, offsite construction modules, and sustainable technologies such as solar thermal systems and water management solutions. With a strong emphasis on innovation and environmental sustainability, Kingspan has seen rapid growth in segments like data center infrastructure and renewable energy products, driven by increasing demand for green building practices and energy conservation globally. This expansion has bolstered the company's presence across Europe, North America, and Asia, positioning it as a key player in the construction and energy efficiency sectors, while maintaining a commitment to reducing carbon footprints through advanced materials and technologies. It is the International Depository Receipt (IDR) market's 7th largest Building materials company by market capitalization.

### 21. [I run the first forward-deployed engineer club in New York. Here's how to break into the role.](https://www.businessinsider.com/forward-deployed-engineer-how-to-get-job-careers-advice-ai-2026-11)
- **Outlet(s):** Business Insider | **Published:** `2026-11-07` | **Sentiment:** `POSITIVE` | **Positioning Relevance Score:** `0.72`
- **Syndicated URLs:** [link](https://ct.moreover.com/?a=60796778802&p=21l&v=1&x=s5Kb6bbzfV8rRMM_Yvn-0w)
- **Matched Innodata Pillars:** Software Engineering
- **Matched Competitors:** Palantir
- **Coverage Snippet:** •Patrick Kelly, the founder of an FDE community, says demand for the role is growing.

•Kelly said that FDEs need to quickly master AI, software engineering, and new industries.

•Kelly offers tips on how to break into an FDE role, even if someone has little technical background.

This as-told-to essay is based on a conversation with Patrick Kelly, the Brooklyn-based cofounder and CEO of Port of Context, which helps companies connect AI to their existing systems. Kelly founded Forward Deployed Engineers of New York in June 2026, which frequently hosts events and has a list of FDE job openings on its website. The conversation has been edited for length and clarity.

I was a forward-deployed engineer before I ever had the title.

I started out as a solutions engineer at a legal AI company, before large language models took off. I'd go into a big company, figure out where AI could help, and work with the people there to build something they could actually use.

When we started Forward Deployed Engineers of New York, a few friends left tech companies to work at fast-growing startups. There's a lot of pressure on you when you're the one going into a customer's business and figuring out what to build, and we thought people could use a place to trade notes.

When people talk about a tech job recession, we have actually seen the opposite in this specific role. Here are some of the latest hiring trends in FDE roles and how people of different backgrounds could all move into the job.

The job finally got a name everyone agrees on

Solutions engineer, solutions architect, customer engineer, implementation engineer, field engineer - I have a whole list of names of what this role used to be called.

While the job description did not change, the explosion in AI subscriptions helped make the job more visible. People started chatting with Claude and pretty quickly found out that getting from a chatbot to a system a business can trust to run on its own is hard. That's where an FDE comes in.

The role is different from that of a traditional software engineer. You go into a big company and figure out where AI could actually help. You work with the customer's engineers to get access to the systems and with the business team to figure out how you'll measure the value. You might spend a few weeks or a few months writing code and building the workflow, and then you hand it over before you leave.

New hiring trends in FDE

Many new opportunities and FDE-adjacent roles are opening up.

I have an analogy for FDEs: You have to be a jack-of-all-trades and a master of three. Those are AI, software engineering, and learning a new business domain fast. That last one is basically like being a McKinsey consultant. Each of those things could be a career on its own.

New York has AI companies hiring FDEs and has a whole lot of places to deploy them. Banks and other big companies have seen how the work gets done, so now some banks are building internal teams to run those projects themselves without relying on the AI companies.

Big consulting firms are also changing how they deliver AI projects.

I'm seeing very technical consultants from places like McKinsey and Bain find a path into forward-deployed engineering. There's also a role for people who haven't spent time as software engineers. At my company, we're pairing FDEs with deployment strategists, which is a role first invented by Palantir.

While the FDE does the coding and technical work, a deployment strategist works with the customer and keeps the technical project moving as a manager. You have to speak the language, understand the AI product, and be very good at delivering for clients.

Landing the job

If you're a software engineer who's never worked directly with a customer, think about a project you got off the ground inside your own company. Maybe you spotted a multimillion-dollar opportunity, figured out how long it would take, and pulled together the team to do it. That's a good story to tell in an interview.

It is also important to explain the project in terms the business cares about. What was the opportunity? How did you get people on board? What did the work deliver? If you can point to a return on the investment, bring that number.

Ultimately, the rise of FDE is a reflection of where the job market is going.

Agents are doing more of the coding now, and teams can deliver results faster. That puts more weight on deciding what to do and how to do it. Being able to explain what you chose to build, why it was worth doing, and what the business got out of it matters more than technical skills.

UNITED STATES EDITION

06 November 2026 11:50 EST

NEW YORK, Nov. 05, 2026 -- ExlService Holdings, Inc. (NASDAQ:EXLS) completes its acquisition of Aimpoint Digital, a data and AI consulting and platform engineering firm. Financial terms were not disclosed. Aimpoint Digital brings forward-deployed strategists and engineers with deep experience across Anthropic, Databricks, and Snowflake, enabling EXL to scale AI workloads across more workflows and functions. The deal adds about 250 AI engineers, platform architects, analytics consultants, and AI strategists, expanding EXL's forward-deployed talent and placing senior AI expertise closer to client teams. The combined organization will deliver continuity from strategy through architecture, engineering, deployment, and managed AI operations, while expanding EXL's partner ecosystem with Anthropic, Databricks, and Snowflake. The acquisition follows EXL's earlier purchase of iMerit to broaden its ability to support clients from strategy to operational AI deployments

- **Coverage Snippet:** AMERICAN DAILY STOCK REPORT

UNITED STATES EDITION

06 November 2026 13:41 EST

NEW YORK, Nov. 05, 2026 — EXL Service Holdings, Inc. (NASDAQ: EXLS) has acquired Aimpoint Digital, a data and AI consulting firm recognized for building enterprise AI platforms with deep ties to Anthropic, Databricks, and Snowflake. The acquisition pairs Aimpoint Digital’s hands-on, forward-deployed AI engineering with EXL’s industry knowledge and scale to advance enterprise AI transformation.

Financial terms were not disclosed. The deal is designed to deepen EXL's ability to guide clients from AI strategy to production, leveraging Aimpoint Digital's platform expertise and EXL's data foundations and partner ecosystem.

The move adds approximately 250 AI engineers, platform architects, analytics consultants, decision scientists, and AI strategists to EXL’s capabilities, expanding the company’s reach across multi-year transformations and accelerating production on key platforms such as Anthropic, Databricks, and Snowflake. The acquisition follows EXL's earlier purchase of iMerit to extend its AI strategy, architecture, and managed operations capabilities

Wed Sep 30, 2026: ExlService Reports Significant Sustainability Advances in Latest Report

ExlService Holdings, Inc. [NASDAQ: EXLS] has published its seventh annual Sustainability Report, titled 'Embedding Sustainability,' showcasing significant advancements in corporate social responsibility (CSR), emissions reductions, and responsible AI governance. The report, released on 30th September 2026, highlights EXL's commitment to integrating sustainability into its core business practices as a means to enhance long-term value for stakeholders.

In the report, dated 2026-10-01 16:41:39.309490, EXL reveals a remarkable 64% reduction in Scope 1 and 2 greenhouse gas emissions from its 2019 baseline. This achievement is complemented by an impressive 78% of the global workforce participating in various CSR initiatives, reflecting the company's dedication to fostering a culture of community engagement and environmental stewardship.

Ajay Ayyappan, executive vice president and general counsel at EXL, stated, 'At EXL, sustainability is not a side initiative; it is central to how we build a stronger, more resilient business.' He emphasized that the report embodies the company's ethos of continuous improvement, demonstrating measurable progress across all facets of its sustainability agenda, including a robust increase in community investment and the maturation of its Responsible and Secure AI program.

The 2025 Sustainability Report adheres to the Global Reporting Initiative Standards and the Sustainability Accounting Standards Board Software and IT Services Standard, ensuring transparency and accountability. Additionally, it aligns with the recommendations of the Task Force on Climate-related Financial Disclosures and maps to the United Nations Sustainable Development Goals, further solidifying EXL's commitment to global sustainability efforts.

EXL remains focused on propelling enterprises to enhance their business models through data and AI, with an emphasis on sustainable practices. As the company continues to innovate and lead in responsible corporate governance, the insights from this report may be of particular interest to investors looking to align their portfolios with companies demonstrating strong environmental, social, and governance (ESG) practices.

Wed Sep 9, 2026: EXL Launches ‘Go Beyond’ Brand, Emphasizing Data-Driven Transformation

EXLService Holdings, Inc. (NASDAQ:EXLS) has unveiled its new brand, ‘Go Beyond,' which encapsulates the company's commitment to innovation and transformation in the realms of data and artificial intelligence. This announcement comes as EXL celebrates its 20th year as a publicly traded entity, with the brand launch taking place on 10th September 2026 at a Nasdaq opening bell ceremony. The new branding aims to reflect EXL's unique position at the intersection of data, AI, industry context, and enterprise execution, driving measurable outcomes for its clients.

Rohit Kapoor, chairman and chief executive officer of EXL, emphasized that ‘Go Beyond' is not just a tagline but a daily standard for the company. He stated, "Our clients come to us to fundamentally reimagine how their enterprises operate. With AI transforming every industry, we've built something rare; a company with domain depth to know what questions to ask and data and AI capabilities to answer them at scale." This strategic shift aims to further solidify EXL's standing as a leader in leveraging data and AI for business transformation.

The rebranding initiative aligns with EXL's foundational purpose of finding better ways to serve its clients and symbolizes its ongoing growth and adaptability. Since its foray into data and analytics in 2006, EXL has consistently pushed the boundaries of traditional industry norms. This includes the recent acquisition of iMerit in August 2026, which has expanded its capabilities in advanced AI model training and frontier AI development. Today, over 60% of EXL's revenues are derived from data and AI-driven services, showcasing its market-leading growth trajectory.

Shirley Macbeth, chief marketing officer of EXL, remarked, "For 27 years, EXL has been built on a simple idea: We find a better way. ‘Go Beyond' is the brand that captures that. It's an invitation to our clients and employees to push the boundaries of what's possible and a reflection of the company EXL has become, a global data and AI leader that combines deep domain and context expertise with trusted execution to drive outcomes that matter." This statement underscores EXL's focus on fostering an innovative culture that prioritizes client success.

EXL continues to serve a diverse clientele that includes leading corporations across various sectors such as insurance, healthcare, banking, and capital markets. As the company embarks on this new branding journey, stakeholders will be keenly observing how ‘Go Beyond' translates into tangible outcomes and strategic advantages for clients in an increasingly competitive landscape. For more insights into EXL's transformation and the ‘Go Beyond' brand in action, interested parties can visit the company's official website.

Thu Aug 20, 2026: ExlService Holdings Expands Credit Capacity: A New Era for AI Investments?

ExlService Holdings, Inc. has taken a significant step in its financial strategy by entering into a new US$1.00 billion senior secured credit agreement with a syndicate led by PNC Bank, as announced on August 18, 2026. This move replaces its previous Citibank facility and extends revolving and term loan maturities to August 18, 2031.

The new credit facility is designed to provide ExlService greater balance sheet flexibility, allowing the company to fund acquisitions, technology investments, and share repurchases. It features leverage-linked pricing and covenants that impose defined limits on debt and interest coverage, which enhances the company's ability to pursue its ambitious goals in the AI sector.

As ExlService Holdings pivots from traditional outsourcing to a focus on data and AI, the new US$1.00 billion credit line appears to support the ongoing narrative of AI-driven growth. This expanded credit capacity is expected to bolster ExlService's strategy to secure AI-led deal wins while managing the associated risks, particularly those arising from rising wage inflation and compliance costs, which could complicate pricing power.

The recent acquisition of iMerit, which has brought its CEO into ExlService's executive team, further underscores the importance of scaling AI data services. Together with the larger credit facility, this acquisition highlights the company's commitment to enhancing its capabilities in this high-demand sector, while also raising concerns related to execution risks in integrating new talent and maintaining resilient operating margins.

Analysts are optimistic about ExlService Holdings' growth potential, projecting revenues of approximately US$3.2 billion and earnings of around US$374.5 million by 2029. The new credit facility could reinforce this growth outlook; however, if cost pressures intensify more than anticipated, it may lead to a reassessment of the feasibility of meeting these targets.

SECTION 2 CORPORATE PROFILE

2.1 Activities

ExlService Holdings, Inc., a Nasdaq-listed company, is a global leader in data analytics, digital operations, and consulting services, primarily serving industries such as insurance, healthcare, banking, and retail. The company's core business revolves around leveraging advanced analytics, artificial intelligence, and machine learning to optimize operations, enhance decision-making, and drive efficiency for its clients. By transforming complex data into actionable insights, ExlService helps organizations streamline processes and mitigate risks. Among its fastest-growing segments are analytics-driven solutions and digital transformation services, fueled by increasing demand for AI-powered tools and cloud-based platforms. This focus has enabled the company to expand its client base and achieve strong revenue growth, positioning it as a key player in the evolving landscape of business process outsourcing and technology-enabled services. ExlService maintains a commitment to innovation and client-centric strategies, fostering sustainable growth in a competitive market. It is the NASDAQ's 6th largest Business services company by market capitalization.

06 November 2026 00:44 EST

NEW YORK, Nov. 05, 2026 -- ExlService Holdings, Inc. (NASDAQ:EXLS) has completed its acquisition of Aimpoint Digital, a data and AI consulting and platform engineering firm known for building enterprise data and AI platforms with deep expertise in Anthropic, Databricks, and Snowflake. Financial terms were not disclosed. The combination strengthens EXL's capacity to support clients across the full AI transformation lifecycle and deepens its relationships with technology partners. Aimpoint Digital brings forward-deployed strategists and engineers who accelerate moving workloads to leading platforms, enabling broader enterprise transformations. The deal adds approximately 250 experienced AI engineers, platform architects, analytics consultants, decision scientists, and AI strategists to EXL's capabilities, expanding its forward-deployed talent base and placing senior AI expertise closer to client teams. The partnership ecosystem with Anthropic, Databricks, and Snowflake expands, enabling faster deployment to production and broader platform coverage. This acquisition builds on EXL's recent purchase of iMerit, further extending its ability to support clients from strategy and architecture through engineering, deployment, and managed AI operations. EXL is a global data and AI company with over 74,000 employees spanning six continents
